PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are visiting San Francisco for the day and want to meet as many friends as possible. Solve the problem by considering various different schedules and picking the best one to optimize your goals.

Travel distances (in minutes):
Marina District to Mission District: 20.
Mission District to Marina District: 19.

CONSTRAINTS: You arrive at Marina District at 9:00AM. Stephanie will be at Mission District from 10:30AM to 1:30PM. You'd like to meet Stephanie for a minimum of 120 minutes.

Your response should start with 'SOLUTION:'.
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are visiting San Francisco for the day and want to meet as many friends as possible. Solve the problem by considering various different schedules and picking the best one to optimize your goals.

Travel distances (in minutes):
Marina District to Mission District: 20.
Mission District to Marina District: 19.

CONSTRAINTS: You arrive at Marina District at 9:00AM. Stephanie will be at Mission District from 10:30AM to 1:30PM. You'd like to meet Stephanie for a minimum of 120 minutes.

Your response should start with 'SOLUTION:'.
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain sf_visit)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types location time person count)
  (:predicates
    (at ?l - location)
    (time_now ?t - time)
    (meeting_progress ?p - person ?v - count)
    (next_t ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (next_v ?v1 ?v2 - count)
    (travel_marina_mission ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (travel_mission_marina ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (available ?p - person ?t - time)
    (met_goal ?p - person)
  )

  ;; Advance time by 1 minute while staying at the same location
  (:action wait
    :parameters (?l - location ?t1 - time ?t2 - time)
    :precondition (and 
      (at ?l) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (next_t ?t1 ?t2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2)
    )
  )

  ;; Move from Marina to Mission (20 minutes)
  (:action move_marina_to_mission
    :parameters (?t1 - time ?t2 - time)
    :precondition (and 
      (at marina) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (travel_marina_mission ?t1 ?t2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at marina)) 
      (at mission) 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2)
    )
  )

  ;; Move from Mission to Marina (19 minutes)
  (:action move_mission_to_marina
    :parameters (?t1 - time ?t2 - time)
    :precondition (and 
      (at mission) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (travel_mission_marina ?t1 ?t2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at mission)) 
      (at marina) 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2)
    )
  )

  ;; Meet Stephanie for 1 minute at the Mission
  (:action meet_stephanie
    :parameters (?t1 - time ?t2 - time ?v1 - count ?v2 - count)
    :precondition (and 
      (at mission) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (next_t ?t1 ?t2) 
      (available stephanie ?t1) 
      (meeting_progress stephanie ?v1) 
      (next_v ?v1 ?v2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2) 
      (not (meeting_progress stephanie ?v1)) 
      (meeting_progress stephanie ?v2)
    )
  )

  ;; Complete the goal for meeting Stephanie for the minimum duration
  (:action complete_meeting_goal
    :parameters (?p - person)
    :precondition (meeting_progress ?p v120)
    :effect (met_goal ?p)
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain sf_visit)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types location time person count)
  (:predicates
    (at ?l - location)
    (time_now ?t - time)
    (meeting_progress ?p - person ?v - count)
    (next_t ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (next_v ?v1 ?v2 - count)
    (travel_marina_mission ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (travel_mission_marina ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (available ?p - person ?t - time)
    (met_goal ?p - person)
  )

  ;; Advance time by 1 minute while staying at the same location
  (:action wait
    :parameters (?l - location ?t1 - time ?t2 - time)
    :precondition (and 
      (at ?l) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (next_t ?t1 ?t2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2)
    )
  )

  ;; Move from Marina to Mission (20 minutes)
  (:action move_marina_to_mission
    :parameters (?t1 - time ?t2 - time)
    :precondition (and 
      (at marina) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (travel_marina_mission ?t1 ?t2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at marina)) 
      (at mission) 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2)
    )
  )

  ;; Move from Mission to Marina (19 minutes)
  (:action move_mission_to_marina
    :parameters (?t1 - time ?t2 - time)
    :precondition (and 
      (at mission) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (travel_mission_marina ?t1 ?t2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at mission)) 
      (at marina) 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2)
    )
  )

  ;; Meet Stephanie for 1 minute at the Mission
  (:action meet_stephanie
    :parameters (?t1 - time ?t2 - time ?v1 - count ?v2 - count)
    :precondition (and 
      (at mission) 
      (time_now ?t1) 
      (next_t ?t1 ?t2) 
      (available stephanie ?t1) 
      (meeting_progress stephanie ?v1) 
      (next_v ?v1 ?v2)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (time_now ?t1)) 
      (time_now ?t2) 
      (not (meeting_progress stephanie ?v1)) 
      (meeting_progress stephanie ?v2)
    )
  )

  ;; Complete the goal for meeting Stephanie for the minimum duration
  (:action complete_meeting_goal
    :parameters (?p - person)
    :precondition (meeting_progress ?p v120)
    :effect (met_goal ?p)
  )
)
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------

    t0 t1 t2 t3 t4 t5 t6 t7 t8 t9 t10 t11 t12 t13 t14 t15 t16 t17 t18 t19 t20 t21 t22 t23 t24 t25 t26 t27 t28 t29 t30 t31 t32 t33 t34 t35 t36 t37 t38 t39 t40 t41 t42 t43 t44 t45 t46 t47 t48 t49 t50 t51 t52 t53 t54 t55 t56 t57 t58 t59 t60 t61 t62 t63 t64 t65 t66 t67 t68 t69 t70 t71 t72 t73 t74 t75 t76 t77 t78 t79 t80 t81 t82 t83 t84 t85 t86 t87 t88 t89 t90 t91 t92 t93 t94 t95 t96 t97 t98 t99 t100 t101 t102 t103 t104 t105 t106 t107 t108 t109 t110 t111 t112 t113 t114 t115 t116 t117 t118 t119 t120 t121 t122 t123 t124 t125 t126 t127 t128 t129 t130 t131 t132 t133 t134 t135 t136 t137 t138 t139 t140 t141 t142 t143 t144 t145 t146 t147 t148 t149 t150 t151 t152 t153 t154 t155 t156 t157 t158 t159 t160 t161 t162 t163 t164 t165 t166 t167 t168 t169 t170 t171 t172 t173 t174 t175 t176 t177 t178 t179 t180 t181 t182 t183 t184 t185 t186 t187 t188 t189 t190 t191 t192 t193 t194 t195 t196 t197 t198 t199 t200 t201 t202 t203 t204 t205 t206 t207 t208 t209 t210 t211 t212 t213 t214 t215 t216 t217 t218 t219 t220 t221 t222 t223 t224 t225 t226 t227 t228 t229 t230 t231 t232 t233 t234 t235 t236 t237 t238 t239 t240 t241 t242 t243 t244 t245 t246 t247 t248 t249 t250 t251 t252 t253 t254 t255 t256 t257 t258 t259 t260 t261 t262 t263 t264 t265 t266 t267 t268 t269 t270 - time
    v0 v1 v2 v3 v4 v5 v6 v7 v8 v9 v10 v11 v12 v13 v14 v15 v16 v17 v18 v19 v20 v21 v22 v23 v24 v25 v26 v27 v28 v29 v30 v31 v32 v33 v34 v35 v36 v37 v38 v39 v40 v41 v42 v43 v44 v45 v46 v47 v48 v49 v50 v51 v52 v53 v54 v55 v56 v57 v58 v59 v60 v61 v62 v63 v64 v65 v66 v67 v68 v69 v70 v71 v72 v73 v74 v75 v76 v77 v78 v79 v80 v81 v82 v83 v84 v85 v86 v87 v88 v89 v90 v91 v92 v93 v94 v95 v96 v97 v98 v99 v100 v101 v102 v103 v104 v105 v106 v107 v108 v109 v110 v111 v112 v113 v114 v115 v116 v117 v118 v119 v120 - count

    (meeting_progress stephanie v0)

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
SOLUTION:
To meet Betty, follow these steps:

1. **Travel from Richmond District to Financial District**: Depart from the Richmond District at 9:00 AM and arrive at the Financial District at 9:22 AM (a 22-minute trip).
2. **Wait at Financial District**: Stay at the Financial District from 9:22 AM until Betty arrives at 5:15 PM.
3. **Meet Betty at Financial District**: Conduct the meeting with Betty from 5:15 PM to 6:15 PM, satisfying the required 60-minute meeting duration.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are visiting San Francisco for the day and want to meet as many friends as possible. Solve the problem by considering various different schedules and picking the best one to optimize your goals.

Travel distances (in minutes):

To meet the goal of spending at least 60 minutes with Jessica, follow this schedule:

1.  **Travel to the meeting location:** At 9:00 AM, depart from the Presidio and travel to the Marina District. You will arrive at 9:15 AM.
2.  **Begin meeting Jessica:** At 9:15 AM, start your meeting with Jessica at the Marina District. This first session will last until 9:30 AM (15 minutes total).
3.  **Continue meeting Jessica:** Remain at the Marina District and continue the meeting from 9:30 AM to 9:45 AM (30 minutes total).
4.  **Continue meeting Jessica:** Remain at the Marina District and continue the meeting from 9:45 AM to 10:00 AM (45 minutes total).
5.  **Conclude meeting Jessica:** Finish the final 15-minute block of your meeting at the Marina District from 10:00 AM to 10:15 AM.

By 10:15 AM, you will have successfully met with Jessica for the required 60 minutes.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1

SOLUTION:

To meet William in San Francisco according to your schedule, follow these steps:

1. **Travel from Nob Hill to The Castro**: Depart Nob Hill at 9:00 AM. The journey takes 17 minutes, arriving at The Castro at 9:17 AM.
2. **Wait at The Castro**: Stay at The Castro from 9:17 AM until 12:15 PM to wait for William's arrival.
3. **Meet with William**: Meet William at The Castro starting at 12:15 PM. Continue the meeting until 1:30 PM to satisfy the required 75-minute duration.
